Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上实现闭环执行，任务成功率分别为100.0%和91.0%。在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的WAM基准测试中，块显存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时，显著提升了不同具身模型架构的部署效率，解决了现有运行时无法满足具身部署特殊需求的问题。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [176] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM是一个视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一的流匹配框架联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，在接触丰富的操作任务中达到71.67%的平均成功率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要策略对局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦做出反应，但这些触觉线索在时间上稀疏且在视觉观察中通常不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观察馈入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉变形动力学。

Method: 提出VT-WAM，包含：(1) 非对称混合专家Transformer注意力机制，用于桥接首帧视觉锚点与时间触觉动力学；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导，鼓励动作查询在接触阶段依赖触觉证据。整体采用统一的流匹配框架联合训练。

Result: 在六个真实世界接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率71.67%，超过Fast-WAM 26.67%和OmniVTLA 35.84%。消融实验证明触觉变形动力学建模和接触阶段触觉注意力引导对接触丰富任务均很重要。

Conclusion: VT-WAM通过联合学习视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，并在接触阶段引导注意力机制关注触觉证据，有效提升了接触丰富操作任务的成功率，验证了触觉变形动力学建模的重要性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑盒信息检索系统中，攻击者仅通过观察检索到的文档集合（无排序和相似度分数）即可推断出所使用的嵌入模型，即嵌入推断攻击（EIA）。该方法在包含重排序器的系统中依然有效，并在真实RAG系统中得到验证，同时提出了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的嵌入模型反转攻击通常假设攻击者已知嵌入模型，但在黑盒场景下攻击者无法获取模型信息。本文旨在揭示仅凭检索结果的无序集合，攻击者能否推断出系统所使用的嵌入模型，从而暴露安全风险。

Method: 攻击者从一组已知候选模型中，构造具有区分性的定制查询，通过观察检索系统返回的无序文档集合来识别目标嵌入模型。方法在包含重排序器的系统中进行了测试，并在真实RAG系统上验证了绕过LLM输入过滤的能力。同时评估了相似度阈值等防御措施。

Result: 实验表明，定制查询能够有效区分不同的嵌入模型，即使系统包含重排序器，部分查询仍具有区分性。在真实RAG系统中，精心设计的查询能够绕过LLM对非标准问题的拒绝机制，成功执行模型推断。

Conclusion: 黑盒信息检索系统存在嵌入模型泄露的风险，仅通过检索结果的无序集合即可推断出嵌入模型。相似度阈值可以作为一种有效的缓解策略，但无法完全消除风险。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>


### [178] [Chameleon: Recovering Cyber-Physical Systems from Memory Corruption Attacks via ML Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01356)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Chameleon框架，利用机器学习代理在检测到内存破坏攻击时自动替换受损组件，实现CPS的持续运行而非终止或降级，在7种机器人平台上验证有效（平均R²=0.96）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS攻击检测技术检测到攻击后要么终止/暂停系统（对安全关键任务不可接受），要么用简化默认组件降级或重启系统，缺乏既能维持任务执行又能安全恢复的机制。

Method: 基于LLVM编译器实现，以分区（compartment）为粒度训练ML代理模型，使其近似原始分区行为但不包含相同内存漏洞。检测到攻击时，将受损分区替换为预训练的ML代理。

Result: 在7种机器人（含仿真和真实）上评估：代理模型与原分区行为高度近似（平均R²=0.96），能成功从真实内存破坏攻击中恢复系统并完成任务，性能与内存开销低。

Conclusion: Chameleon提供了一种不中断任务执行、不降级功能的CPS攻击恢复方案，通过ML代理实现安全且高效的系统持续运行。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly deployed in every aspect of our lives and can be compromised through memory corruption vulnerabilities, allowing attackers to hijack the control flow and take over the system. Existing techniques mostly focus on detecting such attacks but respond by terminating or halting execution upon attack detection, which is not acceptable in CPSs used in safety-critical tasks, as interrupted tasks can have catastrophic consequences. Other techniques replace compromised CPS components with simplified defaults that degrade system behavior, or reboot the system upon attack detection.
  We propose Chameleon, a novel framework for automatically recovering CPSs from memory corruption attacks using machine learning (ML)-based surrogates trained at compartment granularity that nearly replicate their original compartments' behavior but do not have the same memory corruption vulnerabilities. Upon attack detection, Chameleon replaces the compromised compartment with its trained surrogate. We implemented Chameleon using the LLVM compiler and evaluated its efficiency and effectiveness on seven different robotic vehicles (RVs), including simulated and real ones. We found that Chameleon can generate surrogates that closely approximate the original compartments (with an average R$^2$=0.96), successfully recover the system despite real-world memory corruption attacks unlike prior approaches, and complete their tasks while incurring low performance and memory overhead.

</details>


### [179] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognitive Firewall，一种主动运行时监控框架，通过在用户与目标LLM之间插入独立监控模型，利用四个分类门（意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门、输出风险门）进行对话级安全评估，采用升级式决策机制。实验结果表明该方法在多个越狱基准上将攻击成功率降至2%以下，最难数据集上降至14%，过拒率仅8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制通常将提示或响应作为独立消息进行评估，无法恢复跨轮累积的恶意意图、验证声称的权限，或检测在对话中分解的 harmful 目标。这导致LLM在多轮策略下可以被诱导产生有害内容。

Method: Cognitive Firewall框架包含四个分类门：1) 意图门——识别请求的操作目标；2) 零信任上下文门——将声称的角色和权限视为未经验证的证据；3) 一致性门——检测跨轮的 escalation 和分解行为；4) 输出风险门——在发布前检查候选响应。门决策通过升级机制而非分数平均进行组合。

Result: 在四个越狱基准和一个良性安全测试集上，Cognitive Firewall显著降低了单轮、多轮、基于权限和人工设计的攻击成功率。三个攻击集上降至2%或以下，最难的人工设计攻击集上降至14%，同时维持8%的过拒率。

Conclusion: 分解式的对话级监督能够改善LLM安全性的主动遏制和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [180] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了生成式AI（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）和联邦学习在入侵检测系统中的应用，涵盖数据增强、异常检测、隐私保护分布式训练等方向，并讨论了合成数据质量、非IID分布、通信效率、对抗风险等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入侵检测系统面临攻击行为演化、真实数据集获取困难、数据不完整、类别不平衡及隐私约束等挑战，生成式AI和联邦学习结合有望克服这些局限。

Method: 采用结构化综述方式，首先总结IDS研究方向（对抗ML、异常检测、IoT-IDS、可解释IDS、基准数据集），然后按模型家族和任务目标分类生成式AI在IDS中的应用（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM），最后综述生成式AI与联邦学习IDS的融合研究。

Result: 生成式AI可支持异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据插补、对抗流量生成和IDS告警解释；联邦学习使分布式IDS训练无需直接共享本地流量数据，适用于隐私敏感和地理分布场景。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战：合成数据质量、真实流量生成、双用对抗风险、非IID客户端分布、通信高效模型共享、联邦IDS基准测试、面向网络安全的专用LLM。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [181] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于根的密码攻击的泛化问题，通过构造显式同构证明在全分裂设置下，这类攻击无法产生新的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答关于根攻击泛化的关键问题，特别是通过构造显式同构来扩展攻击的可能性。

Method: 首先构造了全分裂多项式环与存在已知攻击的多项式环之间的显式同构，证明该同构会扭曲样本使其无法用于区分；然后证明了任何全分裂多项式环之间的同构都必须具有所构造的形式。

Result: 在全分裂设定下，通过显式同构扩展根攻击的方法不会产生新的漏洞。

Conclusion: 基于根的密码攻击在全分裂设置下无法通过构造同构的方式泛化出新漏洞。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [182] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了AI生成工具带来的身份证件伪造新威胁（物理呈现、数字注入、全生成合成），提出了统一的身份验证威胁模型，调查了从传统规则到基础模型的检测方法，揭示了数据集与现实部署之间的持续差距，并通过零样本基准测试发现最强模型APCER值仍超25%，展示了跨域泛化的严重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使得高质量证件伪造和字段级篡改变得极易实现，而现有检测方法所依赖的基准测试无法反映这一真实的威胁水平，导致检测系统面临严重的现实部署风险。

Method: 提出了统一的身份验证威胁模型，涵盖物理呈现攻击、数字注入攻击和GenAI驱动合成三类攻击；系统追踪了从基于规则的启发式方法到取证定位、注入感知流水线、基础模型和少样本框架的检测方法演进；对2019-2025年间公开数据集进行了系统审计，并开展了零样本基准测试。

Result: 识别了"现实差距"的持续存在；发现大语言多模态模型中存在"脚本依赖生成不稳定性"（SDGI）这一非拉丁文字修补中的重复性排版失败模式；零样本测试显示最强模型在安全导向操作条件下APCER值仍高于25%。

Conclusion: 当前检测系统的跨域泛化能力存在显著局限，未来需要构建更具取证根基、隐私保护和法律可追溯性的身份验证系统。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


### [183] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为hamm-grams的新型鲁棒特征，用于恶意软件检测与分类。hamm-gram是固定长度且包含单字符通配符的特殊正则表达式，通过一种新的局部敏感哈希（LSH）和哈希桶内聚类算法高效发现。实验表明，该特征相比传统n-gram特征具有更强的鲁棒性，在恶意软件分类与检测任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的n-gram特征是恶意软件机器学习系统中常用的静态特征，但这类特征本质上具有脆弱性（brittle），容易因微小的代码变异或混淆而失效，因此需要构建更鲁棒的特征来应对恶意软件的持续演化。

Method: 提出hamm-gram特征（固定长度、含单字符通配符的正则表达式）；设计一种新的局部敏感哈希（LSH），使汉明距离小的字符串对更容易产生碰撞；通过哈希桶内聚类算法确定通配符位置，高效发现常见hamm-gram。

Result: 在恶意软件分类与检测任务中，hamm-gram特征相比传统n-gram特征展现出更好的鲁棒性和性能优势。

Conclusion: hamm-gram是一种有效的鲁棒静态特征，能够通过高效的算法在恶意软件检测中取得优于传统n-gram的效果。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [184] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han,Jie Wu,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在机器人系统中的"过度思考"行为，发现攻击者可通过在视觉场景中嵌入精心设计的可读文本作为触发器，即使在严格黑盒设置下也能显著延迟机器人决策（最高达6.96倍），并通过三阶段框架验证了触发器在不同模型间的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在机器人系统中存在过度思考行为（生成冗长推理链导致推理时间过长），这种现象可能被恶意利用：攻击者通过刻意触发过度思考来拖慢受害机器人系统的决策过程，引发各种安全问题。目前缺乏对这种"过度思考引发的减速攻击"的系统性研究。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1）构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；2）基于前缀代理分数进行高效黑盒搜索，并选择少量候选进行完整延迟测量确认；3）在固定触发器池上评估黑盒迁移性，在未见图像和多个LVLM上报告延迟放大倍数和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上，所有触发器均产生大于1.0倍的减速比，最强单触发器可达6.96倍。物理打印的文本触发器仍能造成高达4.74倍的延迟放大。

Conclusion: 发现的可迁移触发器能够在多个LVLM模型之间转移，并持续导致机器人系统显著减速，证实了该攻击的有效性和威胁性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack.
  This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>


### [185] [LIB-TRAP: Standard Cell Library Hardware Trojan Risk Assessment and Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01526)
*Harish Kumar Dharavath,Md Muhtasim Alam Chowdhury,Rozhin Yasaei,Soheil Salehi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准单元库被恶意篡改的新型硬件木马威胁模型。通过将标准单元库中的木马单元替换为激活版本，攻击者可在不改变设计者意图网表的情况下插入任意硬件木马。基于Saed32nm和Sky130nm工艺，将清洁库转化为恶意库，并在AES-128、以太网控制器和WISHBONE DMA等基准设计上验证了该攻击向量的有效性和隐蔽性。实验表明，从面积、功耗等设计特征中提取的数据可用于二进制分类检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注标准单元间空隙中的硬件木马插入，但缺乏对构成数字设计基础的标准单元本身脆弱性的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索标准单元被恶意篡改的新型威胁模型。

Method: 提出一种新型威胁模型，将标准单元视为不可信。设计方获得被篡改的标准单元库，综合出包含失活木马单元的网表；制造过程中，恶意代工厂将失活木马单元替换为激活版本。使用开源和工业EDA工具将Saed32nm和Sky130nm标准单元库转化为恶意库，并在多个基准设计（AES-128、以太网控制器、WISHBONE DMA）上进行综合验证，提取单元总数、面积、动态功耗和静态功耗等特征用于二进制分类。

Result: 成功将清洁标准单元库转化为恶意库，并在多个基准设计中证明了该攻击向量的有效性。从综合后电路提取的设计特征（单元数、面积、动态功耗、静态功耗）可作为二进制分类的输入，用于检测木马感染。

Conclusion: 标准单元库本身存在安全漏洞，可被用于隐藏硬件木马。该攻击向量具有强隐蔽性，设计者难以通过常规手段察觉。研究为硬件安全领域提出了新的威胁方向，提示业界需要关注标准单元级别的安全防护。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities inherent to the fabless semiconductor manufacturing model have significantly increased the risk of malicious Hardware Trojan (HT) insertion, posing severe threats to hardware security. Several HT mitigation and detection strategies have been developed, and existing works explore the insertion of HTs in the space between standard cells in an integrated circuit. However, there is a lack of research into the vulnerabilities posed by the building blocks of most digital designs on the market today, the standard cells. This work investigates a novel threat model in which standard cells are considered untrusted. Our proposed threat model provides the design house with a tampered standard cell library. The intended netlist is synthesized and implemented using the tampered library. During fabrication, a nefarious foundry replaces the library's deactivated HT cells with activated counterparts. Using open-source and industry-standard Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools, existing standard cell libraries, Saed32nm and Sky130nm, are converted into malicious libraries capable of masking the presence of arbitrary HTs from IC designers. The malicious library is then applied and characterized in multiple standard benchmark designs. To demonstrate the efficacy and stealthiness of this standard cell-based attack vector, three benchmark circuits, an AES-128 encryption core, an Ethernet controller, and a WISHBONE DMA engine, were synthesized using both clean and Trojan-infected libraries across Synopsys 32nm and SkyWater 130nm technologies. Design-level features, including total cell count, total area, dynamic power consumption, and static power, were extracted from these synthesized circuits to serve as inputs for binary classification

</details>


### [186] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat是一个面向硬件安全验证的领域专用对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，结合开源EDA工具，在RTL设计上提供上下文感知的安全指导，以87.73%的Faithfulness得分显著优于商用大模型，并通过AES S-Box硬件木马检测案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证是一个多阶段复杂过程，工程师缺乏结构化安全指导，而通用聊天机器人存在幻觉和知识陈旧问题，无法可靠地提供按需安全辅助。

Method: VeriChat采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，三个专用智能体协同工作以最小化幻觉并提升响应的透明度和可靠性；同时集成开源EDA工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys）对用户提供的RTL设计进行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。

Result: VeriChat的Faithfulness得分为87.73%，显著优于领先的专有模型。在AES S-Box IP硬件木马检测案例中，VeriChat通过多轮对话自主识别、模拟并形式化证明了一个隐蔽的密钥泄漏漏洞。

Conclusion: VeriChat作为现有验证工作流的补充而非替代，能够提供上下文感知的安全指导，有效降低幻觉风险，并成功应用于实际硬件安全威胁检测场景。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗攻击对随机森林和XGBoost分类器预测性能及SHAP解释稳定性的影响，提出解释稳定性指数(ESI)用于量化TreeSHAP属性漂移，发现预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是正交维度，需联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗攻击不仅降级分类器预测性能，还会破坏安全分析师依赖的SHAP解释的稳定性，现有研究缺乏对树模型解释稳定性的系统评估指标。

Method: 在四个表格安全数据集上，对随机森林和XGBoost实施五种对抗攻击（含三种黑盒方法），提出解释稳定性指数(ESI)度量TreeSHAP属性在扰动下的漂移，并与鲁棒性指数(RI)在同一[0,1]尺度上对比。

Result: 基于梯度的ZOO攻击在XGBoost上产生退化结果（RI~0.98），而Square Attack揭示真实脆弱性（RI~0.36）；退化扰动仍导致显著属性漂移（XGBoost ESI 0.06-0.16，RF ESI 0.14-0.29），表明预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是不同维度。

Conclusion: 需要联合测量预测鲁棒性和解释稳定性；提出的二维框架（梯度依赖、查询效率）可解释攻击排名并为树集成评估提供实践指导。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [188] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA)的新型后门攻击方法，通过在深度自监督特征的帧级别散布音色信息，生成对人类听觉自然的毒化样本。同时引入Pmeta-TLA，利用元学习和投影冲突梯度(PCGrad)实现一次性嵌入多个后门的训练机制。实验表明该方法在关键词识别任务中相比基线方法具有更高的攻击效能、更好的隐蔽性、鲁棒性和更低的攻击成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究表明后门攻击对语音分类模型构成严重安全威胁，而现有语音触发器容易被深度神经网络防御者检测，因此迫切需要探索更多潜在攻击技术以暴露和防范此类风险。

Method: 提出TLA（音色泄露攻击），在深度自监督特征的帧级别散布音色信息产生自然毒化样本；提出Pmeta-TLA，基于元学习和投影冲突梯度(PCGrad)实现一次性多后门嵌入训练策略。

Result: 在关键词识别任务中，使用多种深度神经网络模型进行数据投毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提策略在攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性方面更优，且攻击成本更低。

Conclusion: TLA和Pmeta-TLA方法有效提升了后门攻击的隐蔽性和多目标攻击能力，对语音安全研究具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [189] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出信任边界语义鸿沟（TBSG）概念，指工件通过语法验证但未能满足接收域安全需求的状况。通过分析75起安全事件，建立四维分析模型（MDTBSG：身份、空间、时间、解释），并开发设计阶段框架TBSAM用于识别、优先排序和缓解TBSG。在SolarWinds/SUNBURST攻击中回溯验证，表明语法验证不足以保证信任边界安全，需显式化信任边界假设以补充安全设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统依赖格式、协议和签名等语法机制来验证跨信任边界的工件，但这些机制仅证明工件结构合规（格式正确、协议合规、已认证），无法证明工件满足接收域所需的语义安全属性。已签名更新或已认证令牌仍可能导致系统被攻破，这种语义层面的安全缺口未被系统化研究。

Method: 1) 收集2014-2025年间75个公开报告的安全事件，在信任边界层面进行分析。2) 基于分析结果，将语义错位归纳为四维分析模型MDTBSG：身份（Identity）、空间（Spatial）、时间（Temporal）、解释（Interpretation）。3) 在此基础上开发TBSAM框架，从设计规约中识别TBSG、进行优先级排序、追踪传播性缺口至其源头边界，并为每个缺口映射候选架构控制措施。4) 通过SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击的回顾性重构验证框架有效性。

Result: 建立了MDTBSG四维分析模型，能够系统化描述信任边界处的语义错位。开发了TBSAM设计阶段框架，可识别、优先排序、追踪和缓解TBSG。在SolarWinds/SUNBURST案例中，TBSAM成功将接收域假设显式化，区分本地产生与传播性缺口，并识别出能够中断攻击路径的控制措施。

Conclusion: 语法验证在信任边界处是必要的但不够充分。将信任边界假设显式化可以有效补充Security-by-Design方法。研究揭示了当前安全机制中一个被忽视的系统性弱点——语义层面的安全验证缺失，并为解决该问题提供了实用的分析框架和缓解策略。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [190] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于密封委托的液态民主机制，利用去中心化定时释放加密隐藏委托选择以防止羊群效应和胁迫，同时引入排名多委托和个人回退选票应对代表失败。在多个真实数据集上的评估表明，委托提升代表性的效果遵循可恢复缺口定律，密封形成主要降低权力集中度，排名多委托显著减少投票损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液态民主部署采用透明委托，导致选民易受羊群效应影响、胁迫可验证、以及高支持度代表弃权时系统脆弱。本文旨在解决知情专业路由与系统稳健性之间的张力。

Method: 采用去中心化定时释放加密实现密封委托（隐藏委托选择直至计票阶段），扩展协议引入排名多委托和个人回退选票机制，形式化证明了揭示前保密性和重新提交收据无关联性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集（市政参与式预算选举、20个参与式预算选举、6万名美国选民）上的评估发现：委托提升代表性遵循可恢复缺口定律（仅当弃票率高且系统性无代表性时有效）；密封形成主要降低权力集中度而非直接提升准确性；排名多委托与个人回退选票可显著减少投票损失。

Conclusion: 液态民主的优势有条件性，密封委托机制可有效缓解现有透明委托的关键缺陷（羊群效应、胁迫、权力集中、代表失败），为安全液态民主的实际部署提供了理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [191] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln是一个自动化框架，将智能合约漏洞检测转化为程序性知识进化问题，仅需极少标注样本即可合成和优化检测逻辑，在五种真实漏洞类型上达到71%宏平均F1分数，且进化后的知识可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测方法存在局限：基于提示的方法依赖人工设计的检测规则，而微调方法需要大量标注数据，但智能合约漏洞标注样本 inherently 稀缺。作者旨在解决如何自动生成结构化的漏洞检测程序性知识这一核心问题。

Method: 提出EvoVuln框架，包含两大核心机制：(1) 基于控制反转(IoC)架构的运行时，将检测规则编译为可执行策略，解耦确定性控制流与LLM语义推理，产生密集诊断遥测用于精确定位错误；(2) 两阶段进化管线：冷启动阶段利用自动合成的边界用例引导初始规则生成并对其进行压力测试，少样本进化阶段仅用每种漏洞类型5个脆弱+5个安全样本将策略锚定于真实语义。

Result: 在五种真实漏洞类型上达到71%宏平均F1分数，优于所有基线方法。进化后的程序性知识具有可迁移性：使轻量低成本的模型超越大型零样本模型19个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移至其他LLM，一次性进化成本低于50美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln成功将漏洞检测重新定义为程序性知识进化问题，通过IoC架构和两阶段进化管线，仅需极少标注样本即可自动合成高精度的检测逻辑，且具备良好的跨模型可迁移性和成本效益。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>


### [192] [Has This Checkpoint Been Abliterated? A Two-Signal Audit and Its Failure Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01854)
*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需阈值的检查点审计方法，通过结合参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙和权重恢复能量两个内部信号，在部署前检测开源权重检查点的拒绝机制是否被移除。在273个检查点数据集上达到AUROC 0.95，显著优于单一信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时防护措施只能评分生成内容而非检查点本身，无法在部署前判断开放权重检查点的拒绝机制是否被剥离（abliteration），迫切需要一种低成本、高效的预部署审计方法。

Method: 结合两个廉价内部信号：(1) 参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙（activation refusal-gap），提供拒绝特异性；(2) 基模型到候选模型权重差异的权重恢复能量（weight-recovery energy），提供召回率。两者负相关且标签互补，通过z分数和进行无阈值审计。

Result: 在包含Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama、Gemma的273个检查点注册表上，z和分离57个公开abliterations和37个良性微调/合并/指令微调，AUROC达0.95，优于单一信号（0.84和0.90）。Youden校准阈值转移到保留家族时平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检4/57。

Conclusion: 该审计是有效的分流工具而非防篡改方案：依赖于经过认证的参考模型，其声称的有效性受限于评估所用的注册表。映射出两种失败模式：模拟参考可在无训练下绕过两个轴（ΔW=0, ρ=1）；白盒攻击者可训练检查点越过阈值同时保持不安全且连贯。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>


### [193] [Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02121)
*William Hackett,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个黑盒护栏侦察方法，通过监控HTTP、词法和时序信号，能在零先验知识下以100%准确率检测AI系统中是否存在安全护栏，并以98% F1分数区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对AI系统进行黑盒对抗模拟时，研究者难以区分护栏拦截与LLM自身拒绝，而这两者需要不同的绕过技术，影响攻击策略选择与优化。

Method: 仅利用黑盒访问权限，通过监控HTTP响应、词法和时序信号等行为特征，检测护栏存在性，并进一步识别护栏拦截的内容类别及区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝。

Result: 护栏检测准确率100%，正常与恶意交互的行为差异统计显著（q<0.001），区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝的F1分数平均达98%。

Conclusion: 该黑盒侦察方法能有效检测护栏存在并准确区分拦截类型，为AI系统安全评估提供了重要工具。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.

</details>


### [194] [Cloak and Detonate: Scanner Evasion and Dynamic Detection of Agent Skill Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02357)
*Zimo Ji,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Xin Wei,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首先通过SkillCloak框架揭示现有LLM编码助手技能扫描器在自适应规避攻击下的脆弱性（绕过率超90%），继而提出基于运行时行为审计的SkillDetonate系统，通过沙箱执行和边界信息流分析实现97%检测率与2%误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码助手依赖第三方技能市场，带来软件供应链攻击面（凭证窃取、代码泄露、后门植入），现有静态扫描器（模式匹配/LLM评判）易被自适应规避攻击绕过，缺乏行为级防御方案。

Method: SkillCloak：两种互补规避策略——结构混淆（重写可见载荷为语义等价形式）与自提取技能打包（安装时隐藏恶意组件，执行时恢复）。SkillDetonate：行为中心运行时审计器，在沙箱中执行技能，通过OS边界信息流证据（按需闭包提升+标记基污点分析）检测恶意效果。

Result: SkillCloak：SFS打包绕过8种扫描器超90%，结构混淆在多数静态扫描器超80%，混合扫描器达96%。SkillDetonate：检测97%攻击（2%误报率），真实恶意技能维持87%检测率。

Conclusion: 基于外观的审计不足，需转向运行时行为审计。SkillDetonate通过沙箱执行和信息流跟踪提供了有效防御方案。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly rely on third-party agent skills from public marketplaces, which execute with the agent's privileges and create a software supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill can steal credentials, exfiltrate source code, or install backdoors. Existing defenses use static skill scanners based on pattern matching or LLM-as-judge analysis, but it remains unclear whether they withstand adaptive evasions that preserve malicious behavior while changing payload appearance.
  This paper first presents an adversarial study of existing skill scanners through SkillCloak, a payload-preserving evasion framework that keeps the attack semantics intact while transforming their visible form. SkillCloak uses two complementary strategies: Structural Obfuscation, which rewrites visible payload indicators into semantically equivalent forms, and Self-Extracting Skill (SFS) Packing, which hides malicious components from the install-time view and restores them during agent execution. Across eight scanners and 1,613 in-the-wild malicious skills, SFS Packing bypasses every scanner at over 90%, while Structural Obfuscation bypasses over 80% on most static scanners and reaches 96% on a hybrid scanner, showing that appearance-based auditing is insufficient.
  Motivated by this finding, we propose SkillDetonate, a behavior-centric runtime auditor that executes skills in a sandbox and detects malicious effects through OS-boundary information-flow evidence rather than install-time appearance. SkillDetonate combines on-demand closure lift, which observes instructions materialized during execution, with marker-based taint analysis, which tracks sensitive-data flows across the agent context, files, processes, and network operations. The results show that SkillDetonate detects 97% of attacks at a 2% false-positive rate and sustains 87% detection on real-world malicious skills.

</details>


### [195] [SoK: A Taxonomy for Cybersecurity Incident Response Influence Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02451)
*Thomas Biege,Marius Brockhoff,Jonas Kaspereit,Fabian Ising,Lea Gröber,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物，构建了"网络安全事件响应影响因素分类体系（CIR-IF Taxonomy）"，将1999年至2024年中期的知识系统化，并与七个学术框架及NIST SP 800-61r3进行对比，证明该分类体系在事件响应驱动因素方面提供了更丰富、更严谨、更系统的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管网络安全事件响应相关文献不断增长，但仍缺乏统一框架来系统组织现有知识，且事件响应涉及技术、人机交互、组织理论和人因等多个领域，需要综合性整合视角以识别研究空白并更有效地开展实证与理论探索。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，涵盖417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物，基于实证发现推导出CIR-IF分类体系，并与七个既有学术框架及NIST网络安全框架（SP 800-61r3）的事件响应配置文件进行系统性比较。

Result: CIR-IF分类体系提供了比既有框架更丰富、更严谨、更系统化的事件响应影响因素视图，覆盖了技术、人机交互、组织理论和人因等多维度因素，并成功整合了1999年至2024年中期的实证研究成果。

Conclusion: CIR-IF分类体系是首个系统整合网络安全事件响应多领域影响因素的综合框架，有助于研究者识别未充分探索的领域，为未来实证和理论研究提供更有针对性的指导。

Abstract: Cybersecurity incident response has emerged as a critical area of interest for both researchers and practitioners. The corpus of literature on cybersecurity incident response is expanding, yet a unified framework for systematically organizing the accumulated knowledge remains absent. The aspects of incident response span multiple domains, including technology, human-computer interaction, organizational theory, and human factors. A comprehensive, integrative perspective on these factors can enable researchers to identify underexplored areas and more effectively target their empirical and theoretical investigations. Our study systematizes the factors that influence organizational preparedness for and response to cybersecurity incidents. Through a systematic review of academic literature (n = 417) and non-scientific publications (n = 40), we derived the "Cybersecurity Incident Response Influencing Factor Taxonomy" (\textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy}). Existing empirical findings were classified within this taxonomy, providing a comprehensive and up-to-date overview of knowledge from the period 1999 to mid-2024. The taxonomy categories were systematically compared with seven established scientific frameworks and with the \textit{NIST Cyber Security Framework} elements referenced in the \textit{NIST Special Publication 800-61r3} incident response profile. The results of this comparison show that the \textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy} delivers a richer, more rigorous, and more systematically organized view of the factors that drive and shape incident response.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [CADENZA in Action: Breaking the Monolith with Intent-Dependent Plan Spaces for Semantic Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01468)
*Jaehyun Ha,Yongjoo Park,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CADENZA是一个语义查询优化器，通过将自然语言意图分解为多个步骤，为每个步骤选择具体的物理实现并调优参数，在用户指定的质量-延迟-成本偏好下优化语义查询处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义查询优化器以粗粒度方式优化操作符（如LLM或嵌入模型），导致昂贵的模型调用与廉价的替代方案之间存在权衡，且无法捕捉意图依赖的语义。

Method: CADENZA将意图编译为分解步骤，为每个步骤选择具体物理实现，并在用户指定的质量-延迟-成本偏好下调优参数。

Result: 通过Web界面展示在多模态数据库上的语义查询优化过程，包括意图分解为替代计划、计划优化以及不同偏好下的最优计划选择。

Conclusion: CADENZA通过细粒度的步骤分解和参数调优，有效解决了语义查询处理中粗粒度优化带来的质量-成本权衡问题。

Abstract: Semantic query processing engines execute semantic operators, whose behavior is specified by natural-language intents, via model inference over multimodal data. Most existing optimizers optimize the operators at the granularity of monolithic implementations -- such as LLMs and embedding models -- forcing a trade-off between expensive model calls and cheaper alternatives that fail to capture intent-dependent semantics. We present CADENZA, a semantic operator optimizer that compiles an intent into decomposed steps, selects concrete physical implementations for each step, and tunes their parameters under user-specified quality-latency-cost preferences. In this demonstration, users interact with CADENZA through a web interface over multimodal databases, exploring how an intent is decomposed into alternative plans, how each plan is optimized, and how different preferences yield different winning plans.

</details>


### [197] [The General Stability of Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01546)
*Houming Chen,H. V. Jagadish*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出"一般稳定性"概念，将排名稳定性从精确匹配推广为基于距离的度量，并针对不同距离函数设计了计算算法，包括二维扫描算法、无偏多维采样器以及针对拟凸距离函数的Conv-SC算法，实验验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排名稳定性方法（体积法）要求权重变化后排名必须完全不变才视为稳定，过于严苛——轻微权重变化导致低排名项互换即被判定为不稳定。实际应用中，用户更关心特定排名变化（如榜首变动），而非所有变化同等对待。

Method: 提出一般稳定性（general stability），引入用户自定义距离函数聚合排名区域；设计二维扫描算法和无偏多维采样器（扩展精确稳定性方法）；针对拟凸距离函数提出Conv-SC算法，将稳定性计算归约为凸体积近似问题，可利用随机多项式时间方法求解。

Result: 在8个真实数据集和生成数据上的实验表明：距离敏感稳定性能提供有信息量的结果；所提估计器准确且实用；Conv-SC在拟凸距离函数下显著改善了随维度增长的扩展性。

Conclusion: 一般稳定性以距离函数为灵活性工具，克服了精确匹配的刚性缺陷，且通过拟凸距离函数子类的凸体积归约，在计算可扩展性上取得突破，为排名稳定性分析提供了更实用、更具表达力的框架。

Abstract: Rankings derived from weighted scoring functions are widely used in settings such as university rankings and employment candidate evaluations. Since ranking weights are often chosen by organizations or analysts, ranking stability asks whether a reported ranking persists under reasonable weight changes. Prior work on stable rankings formalizes this idea through volume-based stability, which measures the fraction of the weight space that induces the target ranking exactly.
  This exact-match requirement can be too blunt: once a perturbed weight vector produces a different ranking, exact stability gives it no credit, whether the change replaces the top-ranked item or only swaps two nearly tied lower-ranked items. We propose general stability, a distance-based generalization that aggregates ranking regions according to a user-defined distance from the target ranking. This lets users specify which ranking changes matter in the application, while recovering exact stability as a special case.
  Our algorithmic focus is stability computation: given a reported or user-specified ranking and a distance function, estimate its general-stability score. We give a two-dimensional sweep algorithm and an unbiased multidimensional sampler that extend exact-stability methods, and analyze why sampling can scale poorly as the dimension grows. Motivated by this scaling challenge, we identify quasiconvex distance functions as a tractable subclass and introduce Conv-SC, which reduces stability computation for this subclass to convex-volume approximation, where randomized polynomial-time methods are available. Experiments on eight real datasets and generated instances show that distance-sensitive stability gives informative real-data results, that our estimators are accurate and practical, and that Conv-SC improves scaling with dimension for quasiconvex distance functions.

</details>


### [198] [pykci: A Compact Urban Knowledge Graph for Semantic and Spatial Queries using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01605)
*Huynh Duc An Son Nguyen,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: pykci是一个开源系统，能将CityGML 2.0数据集转换为Neo4j中的城市知识图谱，并通过大语言模型支持自然语言查询，实现了语义3D城市模型的可交互查询，无需用户掌握查询语言和数据库模式知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CityGML作为OGC标准虽能描述丰富的语义3D城市模型，但其XML编码和关系数据库映射使得直接查询困难，需要专业知识，限制了城市规划师、GIS从业者和普通市民对语义3D城市模型的使用。

Method: 提出pykci系统：(1) 将CityGML 2.0数据集转换为Neo4j知识图谱，涵盖所有主题特征模块和细节层次，并用R树进行空间索引；(2) 提供端到端Python流水线，支持数据导入、OGC 3D Tiles可视化及无损回导出CityGML；(3) 采用模型无关的text-to-Cypher机制，将图模式作为上下文，由LLM将自然语言问题翻译为Cypher查询执行。支持本地开源模型和商业模型两种方案。

Result: 在德国汉堡的开放政府CityGML LoD2数据集上验证，成功处理了包括识别适合绿化的屋顶表面等复杂语义和空间查询。答案基于真实城市数据而非模型参数记忆，减少了幻觉并提供了可审计的出处。

Conclusion: pykci使得城市规划师、GIS从业者和市民无需查询语言和数据库模式专业知识即可与语义3D城市模型进行交互，降低了语义3D城市模型的使用门槛。

Abstract: CityGML, the OGC standard for modeling, storage, and exchange of semantic 3D city models, describes urban objects with detailed semantics, geometry, and topology. Yet this richness is difficult to query directly: CityGML's XML encoding is designed for exchange rather than analysis, and relational mappings expose it through schemas requiring expert knowledge. We present pykci (Python Knowledge Graph for Cities), an open-source system that transforms CityGML 2.0 datasets into a compact urban knowledge graph in Neo4j and makes it queryable in natural language. The graph schema covers all thematic feature modules of CityGML 2.0 across all levels of detail and is spatially indexed with an R-tree for efficient geometric retrieval. A complete end-to-end Python pipeline ingests CityGML datasets into the knowledge graph, exports them to OGC 3D Tiles for interactive visualization, and supports lossless round-trip export of all content back to CityGML. For querying, the graph is paired with a large language model through a model-agnostic text-to-Cypher mechanism: the graph schema is supplied as context, and the model translates natural-language questions into Cypher queries executed against the graph. We evaluate both a locally running open-weight model, which keeps sensitive city data on-premise, and a state-of-the-art commercial model for the most demanding spatial and semantic queries. Answers are grounded in exact city data rather than the model's parametric memory, reducing hallucination and providing auditable provenance for every response. We demonstrate the system on open-government CityGML LoD2 datasets from Hamburg, Germany, including complex semantic and spatial queries such as identifying roof surfaces suitable for greening. pykci enables urban planners, GIS practitioners, and citizens to interact with semantic 3D city models without expertise in query languages and database schemas.

</details>


### [199] [AgenticDataBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01647)
*Zhaoyan Sun,Shan Zhong,Daizhou Wen,Jiaxing Han,Guoliang Li,Ying Yan,Peng Zhang,Yu Su,Xiang Qi,Baolin Sun,Chengyuan Yang,Tao Fang,Huaiyu Ruan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出AgenticDataBench基准，用于评估基于大语言模型的数据智能体在多样化数据科学工作流中的细粒度表现，涵盖15个垂直领域（含5个金融科技真实B2B用例），通过数据科学技能量化基准覆盖度，并基于技能对齐的层次聚类从Stack Overflow提取代表性技能，同时提出系统化的LLM任务生成方法，最终对现有数据智能体进行详细评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺乏能够全面、细粒度评估基于LLM的数据智能体的基准，无法真实反映数据科学工作流的多样性和复杂性。

Method: 1) 收集15个垂直领域（含5个金融科技B2B用例）的真实数据集与任务；2) 引入数据科学技能概念，通过技能对齐的层次聚类从Stack Overflow提取代表性技能，量化基准覆盖度；3) 针对真实业务任务，选择最大化技能组合多样性的任务-解决方案对；4) 针对缺乏真实任务的领域，提出基于LLM的系统化任务生成方法；5) 开源测试平台并评估现有数据智能体。

Result: 构建了覆盖多领域、细粒度标注的AgenticDataBench基准，并在开源测试平台上对多种数据智能体进行了技能级别的详细评估。

Conclusion: AgenticDataBench为评估LLM数据智能体提供了更全面、细粒度的基准，有助于推动自动化数据科学的发展。

Abstract: Data science aims to derive actionable insights from heterogeneous raw data, unlocking the value of the massive amounts of data generated in modern society. Automating this process is essential to reducing labor-intensive efforts for data scientists and enabling scalable data-driven applications. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based data agents have emerged as a promising solution to automate data science workflows. However, the field lacks comprehensive benchmarks to rigorously evaluate these agents across diverse scenarios with fine-grained granularity. To address this gap, we propose AgenticDataBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring realistic tasks spanning diverse domains with fine-grained ground-truth labels. This enables evaluations to capture the diversity and complexity of data science workflows and the detailed performance of agents. First, to cover diverse domains, we collect real datasets and tasks from 15 vertical domains, including 5 real-world B2B use cases from a leading fintech company. Second, to remove redundancy in real-world tasks and generate high-quality tasks for domains lacking real data, we introduce data science skills, recurring data-centric operational patterns, and quantify benchmark coverage by the number of skills included. Representative skills are extracted from large-scale task solutions on Stack Overflow using skill-aligned hierarchical clustering. Third, for real-world business tasks, we select task-solution pairs that maximize diversity in skill composition, ensuring broad coverage of practical scenarios. Fourth, to generate realistic tasks for devise domains without real tasks, we propose a systematic LLM-based task generation approach to create workflows and tasks based on these skills. Finally, we evaluate state-of-the-art data agents using our annotated benchmark and open-sourced testbed, providing detailed skill-level insights.

</details>


### [200] [HNSW with Accuracy Guarantees Using Graph Spanners -- A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02338)
*Minghao Li,Raghav Mittal,Sanjivni Rana,Suraj Shetiya,Gautam Das,Nick Koudas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出"Certify-then-Rectify"框架，在HNSW启发式搜索基础上结合无分布统计认证器与极值理论几何因子估计，实现了近似搜索的速度与精确搜索的正确性保证的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNSW图虽为行业标准，但其贪心图遍历是启发式方法，缺乏理论正确性保证。现有方法要么牺牲速度追求精确性，要么放弃理论保证追求效率，两者之间存在空白。

Method: 提出"Certify-then-Rectify"两阶段框架：先通过无分布统计认证器以极小开销动态评估HNSW搜索结果质量；若认证不合格，则安全升级至精确恢复算法。精确恢复阶段将HNSW图重新解释为几何spanner，利用极值理论随机估计其最大经验拉伸因子，从而在数学上界定真实最近邻的最大距离。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，该分层框架在平均情况下保持HNSW的速度，同时确保最坏情况下的精确搜索正确性，且优于其他可行方法。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了启发式近邻搜索的速度优势与精确检索的理论严谨性之间的鸿沟，为高性能向量搜索提供了兼具效率与保证的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) graphs serve as the industry standard due to their logarithmic complexity and strong empirical performance. However, HNSW relies on greedy graph traversal, a heuristic that provides no theoretical guarantees of correctness. In this paper, we propose a novel "Certify-then-Rectify" framework that bridges the gap between the speed of heuristic search and the rigor of exact retrieval. Rather than discarding HNSW, our approach first employs a distribution-free statistical certifier to dynamically evaluate the quality of a standard HNSW search with minimal overhead. If certification indicates that the retrieved neighbors are of low quality, the framework safely escalates to a rigorous exact recovery algorithm. To make this exact recovery computationally feasible, we reinterpret the HNSW graph as a geometric spanner and utilize Extreme Value Theory to stochastically estimate its maximum empirical stretch factor. This allows us to mathematically bound the maximum distance of true nearest neighbors. Extensive evaluations on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our tiered framework delivers the average-case speed of HNSW while ensuring the worst-case correctness of exact search and outperforming other applicable approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Folding an e-graph in pure egglog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01249)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在纯egglog中定义和实现catamorphism（一种通用的折叠操作）的方法，利用e-graph的e-class抽象来获得免费的记忆化和计算缩减，并通过阶段性推理规则避免值的重新传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数式编程中折叠递归数据结构（catamorphism）是核心操作，但现有实现存在冗余计算。将折叠操作引入e-graph上下文可以带来免费记忆化和计算缩减等好处，因此需要探索如何在egglog中实现这一通用折叠机制。

Method: 在纯egglog中定义并实现了catamorphism，采用了一种可复用的实现技巧，即对推理规则进行阶段性划分（staging），从而避免已更新值的重新传播问题。

Result: 成功在egglog中实现了catamorphism，实现了记忆化自动获得和计算量的大幅减少（因为子问题定义在e-class层面而非具体子项层面），并且通过规则阶段性划分解决了值重新传播的问题。

Conclusion: 在e-graph环境下的egglog中实现catamorphism是可行且有益的，所提供的阶段性推理规则技巧可以作为一种可复用的模式，为后续在egglog中实现更复杂的递归计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: Folding over a recursive data structure (also known as "catamorphism") is an essential operation in functional programming. It allows one to declaratively define a recursive computation by focusing on how solutions of subproblems are combined into the solution of the parent problem. Folding in the context of e-graphs can have several benefits. Not only do we get memoization for free, but also computation is drastically reduced since the subproblems are defined over e-classes as opposed to concrete sub-terms. This short report presents our initial attempt at defining and implementing catamorphism, a general notion of a fold, in pure egglog. We discuss a reusable implementation trick to stage the inference rules so that there is no re-propagation of updated values.

</details>


### [202] [Trustworthy Runtime Verification via Bisimulation (Extended Experience Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01363)
*Ryan G. Scott,Ivan Perez,Alwyn E. Goodloe,Mike Dodds,Robert Dockins*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出CopilotVerifier，一个与Copilot运行时验证编译器并行的形式化验证工具，通过生成正确性证明（双模拟关系）来确保生成的C监控程序在语义上等价且不会崩溃，从而为安全关键系统中的监控代码提供高可信度的保证论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键系统中使用运行时验证时，监控代码必须正确运行。Copilot框架从Haskell DSL生成C监控程序，但人类审计者难以确信编译后的监控程序不会崩溃且实现了Copilot语义要求的行为，缺乏有力的保证论证。

Method: CopilotVerifier与Copilot编译器并行运行，生成编译输出的正确性证明。证明采用双模拟关系（bisimulation），分解为一组验证条件（verification conditions）。利用两个基于SMT的技术：Crucible符号执行库（用于LLVM）和What4求解器接口库。

Result: 实验表明，通过构建在现有工具之上，以适中的成本即可大幅提升编译器的可信度，为最终生成令人类审计者信服的形式化保证论证铺平了道路。

Conclusion: CopilotVerifier证明了可以在合理代价下，通过利用现有SMT支撑的形式化工具（Crucible和What4）为安全关键系统中的运行时验证编译器提供显著增强的可信度保证。

Abstract: When runtime verification is used to monitor safety-critical systems, it is essential that monitoring code behaves correctly. The Copilot runtime verification framework pursues this goal by automatically generating C monitor programs from a high-level DSL embedded in Haskell. In safety-critical domains, every piece of deployed code must be accompanied by an assurance argument that is convincing to human auditors. However, it is difficult for auditors to determine with confidence that a compiled monitor cannot crash and implements the behavior required by the Copilot semantics.
  In this paper we describe CopilotVerifier, which runs alongside the Copilot compiler, generating a proof of correctness for the compiled output. The proof establishes that a given Copilot monitor and its compiled form produce equivalent outputs on equivalent inputs, and that they either crash in identical circumstances or cannot crash. The proof takes the form of a bisimulation broken down into a set of verification conditions. We leverage two pieces of SMT-backed technology: the Crucible symbolic execution library for LLVM and the What4 solver interface library. Our results demonstrate that dramatically increased compiler assurance can be achieved at moderate cost by building on existing tools. This paves the way to our ultimate goal of generating formal assurance arguments that are convincing to human auditors.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [BFF: Simple explanations for complex phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01483)
*Charlotte Knierim,Luca Versari,Robert Obryk,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Rif A. Saurous*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“计算生命”论文的观点，提出通过程序空间中的简单突变随机漫步同样容易发现自我复制体，并证明限制祖先树的深度和宽度并不能阻止自我复制体的出现，只是阻止了它们在计算汤中占据主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证与“计算生命”论文不同的假设：自我复制体是否可以通过简单的突变随机漫步在程序空间中被发现，以及限制祖先树参数是否会真正阻止自我复制体的出现。

Method: 利用最新的自我复制体检测技术，在计算汤中进行简单突变随机漫步实验，并与配对交互方法进行对比；同时通过限制祖先树的深度和宽度来观察自我复制体的行为变化。

Result: 简单突变随机漫步至少与配对交互方法同样有效地发现自我复制体；限制祖先树的深度和宽度并不能阻止自我复制体出现，只是阻止了它们在汤中占据主导地位。

Conclusion: 自我复制体的出现并不依赖于特定的交互机制，且限制祖先树参数只能抑制其种群扩张而非根本性消除。

Abstract: The ''Computational Life'' paper (Agüera y Arcas et al., 2024) argues that paired interactions in a computational soup are an effective way to find self-replicators. In this work, aided by recent developments in self-replicator detection, we explore the alternate hypothesis that self-replicators can be found at least as easily using simple mutation random walks in program space. We also explore the claim that capping the maximum ''depth'' and ''width'' of the ancestry tree stops self-replicators from emerging, showing instead that it merely stops self-replicators from taking over the soup.

</details>


### [204] [MMAO-Cls: Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimization for Joint Feature Selection and Classifier Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01539)
*Jinliang Xu,Liping Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了代谢多智能体优化器（MMAO）作为分类模型选择的外环优化器的可行性，提出了MMAO-Cls混合空间实现，在七个表格基准上评估，结果表明其在保持测试性能的同时实现了最紧凑的特征选择，但统计显著性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MMAO能否作为分类模型选择中可信的外环优化器，并验证其混合空间搜索与智能体共享机制的有效性。

Method: 提出MMAO-Cls，每个智能体联合编码二元特征掩码和分类器超参数；将私有能量、公共预算、角色漂移和生命周期映射到包装法学习的准确率-复杂度权衡；引入特征预算自适应和验证奖励正则化（包含子集紧凑性和过拟合差距）。

Result: 在聚合验证目标上排名第二（0.9433）；在测试集上平均得分0.8882，优于RandomSearch（0.8808）和GA-lite（0.8857）；使用最紧凑的特征子集（特征比率0.4881）；但统计检验未达到显著水平。

Conclusion: MMAO-Cls支持分类适用性和紧凑混合空间搜索，但无法证明共享机制是独立的决定性优势，结论较为保守。

Abstract: This paper studies whether the Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer (MMAO) can act as a credible outer-loop optimizer for classification model selection. We propose MMAO-Cls, a mixed-space realization in which each agent jointly encodes a binary feature mask and classifier hyperparameters, while private energy, communal budget, role drift, and lifecycle turnover are mapped to the accuracy-complexity tradeoff of wrapper learning. The implementation is strengthened by deriving feature-budget adaptation from feature-information priors and by regularizing validation reward with both subset compactness and train-validation overfitting gap. We evaluate MMAO-Cls on seven standard tabular benchmarks with three seeds each and compare it against RandomSearch, GA-lite, PSO-lite, and an endogenous no-sharing ablation. On the aggregate validation objective, MMAO-Cls ranks second ($0.9433$) behind GA-lite ($0.9446$). On held-out test performance, it reaches mean score $0.8882$, improving over RandomSearch ($0.8808$) and GA-lite ($0.8857$), remaining close to PSO-lite ($0.8874$) and the no-sharing ablation ($0.8900$), while using the most compact mean held-out feature subset among all compared methods (feature ratio $0.4881$). Pairwise tests show that these margins are not yet statistically significant. The resulting claim is therefore conservative: MMAO-Cls supports classification applicability and compact mixed-space search more clearly than it isolates communal sharing as a decisive standalone advantage.

</details>


### [205] [Mechanism and Stability Analysis of Metabolic Closed-Loop Metaheuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01551)
*Jinliang Xu,Liping Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文从框架层面对代谢多智能体优化器（MMAO）进行理论分析，通过建立通用的状态模型，在温和假设下证明了私有能量、公共预算、角色状态和活跃种群数量的有界性和非负性，并刻画了收缩、再投资和搜索重分配三种内源性行为机制，最终给出一个受限的、可再生的、资源调节的MMAO解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究MMAO中私有能量、公共预算、角色漂移和生命周期更替这一代谢资源循环是否具有超越叙事隐喻的框架级理论解释，即验证该循环是否存在可证明的通用性质。

Method: 构建了一个抽象领域特定移动算子、保留资源记账机制的通用MMAO状态模型；在有限增益和有限支出假设下推导有界性和非负性；识别三种内源性行为机制（资源赤字下的收缩、公共积累盈余下的再投资、异质边际收益下的搜索重分配）；辅以连续和离散MMAO实现的实证验证。

Result: 建立了私有能量、公共预算、角色状态和活跃种群数量的有界性与非负性理论保证；刻画了三种行为机制；区分了循环的通用性质与实现特异性；提供了初步实证支持。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是给出了一个受限的、可再生的、资源调节的MMAO框架解读，而非完整的自适应行为证明；强调了理论分析的保守性和边界意识，不声称全局收敛或普适优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies the Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer (MMAO) at the framework level rather than at the implementation or benchmark level. The central question is whether the metabolic resource loop of private energy, communal budget, role drift, and lifecycle turnover has a framework-level interpretation beyond narrative metaphor. We introduce a generic MMAO state model that abstracts away domain-specific move operators while retaining the resource bookkeeping that defines the framework. Under mild bounded-gain and bounded-spending assumptions, we establish boundedness and nonnegativity properties for private energy, communal budget, role state, and active population size. We then characterize three endogenous behavioral regimes of the loop: contraction under sustained resource deficit, reinvestment under surplus communal accumulation, and search redistribution under heterogeneous marginal returns across agents or subgroups. The analysis is intentionally conservative. It does not claim global convergence of the full adaptive system, universal superiority over specialist optimizers, or a complete stationary characterization of the resulting process. Instead, it identifies which internal regulation properties are generic consequences of the loop and which remain implementation specific. A compact mechanism-validation package on representative continuous and discrete MMAO realizations provides supporting empirical evidence for this reading, but is not intended to replace a full benchmark study. The resulting contribution is therefore a bounded, regenerative, resource-regulated interpretation of MMAO, rather than a complete proof of all adaptive behaviors of the full algorithm family.

</details>


### [206] [Evolutionary Wave Function Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02082)
*Dipika Rajesh,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出将波函数坍缩（WFC）与进化搜索相结合，通过进化WFC的小型输入样例而非直接进化完整关卡来优化程序化内容生成，并在迷宫和塞尔达风格地牢两个领域进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将WFC与进化搜索结合的新范式，通过进化WFC的输入（基因型）间接生成关卡（表型），以克服WFC在全局约束上的局限性。

Method: 使用WFC作为基因型到表型的映射，对WFC的小型输入样例进行进化优化，生成的关卡通过领域特定的适应度函数评估，在迷宫连通性地图和塞尔达风格地牢两个领域进行测试。

Result: 在局部关系决定属性的领域（如迷宫），进化优化WFC输入能提升生成质量；但在需要全局约束的领域（如地牢布局）仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能有效引导WFC生成，但仅当目标目标与局部结构对齐时效果显著。

Abstract: Wave Function Collapse (WFC) is a widely used procedural content generation method that learns local adjacency constraints from example inputs to generate larger outputs. In this paper, we explore combining WFC with evolutionary search by evolving the small input examples used by WFC rather than directly evolving complete levels. In this approach, WFC acts as a genotype-to-phenotype mapping. The generated levels are then evaluated through domain-specific fitness functions. We evaluate the method in two domains with different relationships between local and global structure: Maze connectivity maps and Zelda-style dungeon layouts. Our results show that evolutionary optimization over WFC inputs improves generation quality in domains where properties emerge from local relationships, while domains requiring global constraints remain challenging. These findings suggest that evolutionary search can effectively guide WFC generation when target objectives align with local structure.

</details>


### [207] [Electronic Bursting Neuron: design, equations and hardware implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02122)
*Lev V. Takaishvili,Vladimir I. Ponomarenko,Maksim V. Kornilov,Ilya V. Sysoev*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于锁相环系统电路实现的新型爆发式电子神经元设计，采用混合方法从现象学方程出发，调整简化电路实现，最终得到结构简单、与方程匹配良好的神经元电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子神经元方案在复杂性、实现成本、能展示的动态模式以及数学描述的完备性等方面存在不足，无法同时满足所有需求，因此需要新的设计方案。

Method: 采用新颖的混合方法：从提供所需行为的现象学方程出发，调整和修改这些方程以简化硬件实现，而非直接将生物物理方程映射到硬件或为已有电路编写方程。

Result: 实现了电路实现简单且与底层方程高度匹配的爆发式电子神经元，该电路不仅可以描述单个神经元，还可用于描述小型神经回路。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于锁相环系统的电子神经元设计方案兼具实现简单性和数学描述准确性，可推广至小型神经回路建模，为脉冲神经网络硬件实现提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Electronic neurons are a keystone for construction of the spiking neural networks which have numerous applications in neuroprosthetics, artificial memory, intensive calculations etc. A number of concepts of electronic neurons has been already proposedm with some of them implemented in hardware. However, new schemes are of significant interest since the existing ones do not fit all requirements: either they are too complex and expensive in realization, or they are not able to demonstrate all demanded regimes, or their do not have a appropriate mathematical description and therefore may be investigated only experimentally etc.
  In this study we propose a new design of bursting electronic neuron constructed as a circuit implementation of the equations of a phase-locked loop system. To succeed, we use a novel hybrid approach: we start from the phenomenological equations providing the demanded, then we adjust and modify these equations to simplify the implementation rather than implementing the biophysical equations into thee hardware directly or writing equations for the already constructed circuit. The resulting circuit is simple in implementation and well matches the underlying equations. It can be used for description of not only a single neuron, but small neural circuits too.

</details>


### [208] [Dendritic In-Context Learning in a Single-Layer Spiking Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02283)
*Juwei Shen,Yujie Wu,Changwen Chen*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DendriCL，一种基于单层树突隔室的脉冲神经网络架构，通过将树突隔室本身作为计算基质而非被动管道，利用其亚阈值动力学实现在线Widrow-Hoff LMS算法，从而在无需注意力、深度或推理时可塑性的情况下完成上下文学习，并在超维度Garg-2022基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物 plausible 的脉冲神经网络（SNN）在Garg-2022上下文学习基准测试中，当任务维度较高时表现失败。作者追踪到这一失败源于先前的结构假设：现有SNN设计将适应性路由通过推理时的突触可塑性，并将树突隔室视为误差或教师信号的被动通道。作者质疑这一假设，认为单个树突隔室的亚阈值动力学已经实现了完整的在线学习算法。

Method: 提出DendriCL——一种单层隔室脉冲架构，其顶端循环在结构上等同于泄露在线Widrow-Hoff LMS。通过将树突隔室视为计算基质而非被动管道，将通用ICL所需的架构深度压缩到单层。该方法的更新仅基于动力学，而非训练中隐式发现。

Result: DendriCL在超维度Garg-2022 ICL基准测试中表现出独特的种子稳定性，而密集Transformer在此情况下会出现类似grokking的不稳定性并在中等任务维度以上失败。线性探针可以从顶端膜电位直接恢复参考在线LMS轨迹，R²=0.93，表明该算法在结构上嵌入于动力学中而非训练中隐式发现。

Conclusion: 上下文学习既不需要注意力机制，也不需要深层架构，更不需要推理时的可塑性：一个具有在线LMS动力学的单隔室足矣。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) operates via implicit gradient descent embedded in the forward pass of modern AI architectures -- Transformers, Mamba, state-space models, and MLPs. Capturing this capability in biologically plausible Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) has remained an open challenge: existing SNNs fail the Garg-2022 benchmark at non-trivial task dimensions. We trace this failure to a structural assumption: prior SNN designs route adaptation through inference-time synaptic plasticity, viewing the dendritic compartment as a passive conduit for error or teacher signals. We challenge this assumption. The subthreshold dynamics of a single dendritic compartment already implement a complete online learning algorithm. By treating the compartment as the computational substrate rather than a passive conduit, we propose DendriCL -- a single-layer compartmental spiking architecture whose apical recurrence is structurally identical to leaky online Widrow-Hoff LMS. This dynamics-only update collapses the architectural depth required for general-purpose ICL to a single layer. DendriCL is uniquely seed-stable at super-dimensional Garg-2022 ICL -- where dense Transformers exhibit grokking-style instability and fail past moderate task dimension -- and a linear probe recovers the reference online-LMS trajectory directly from the apical membrane at R^2 = 0.93, showing the algorithm is structurally embedded in the dynamics rather than implicitly discovered during training. Taken together, ICL requires neither attention, depth, nor inference-time plasticity: a single compartment with online-LMS dynamics is sufficient.

</details>


### [209] [Hybridizing a Grouping Metaheuristic with Reinforcement Learning for the One-Dimensional Bin Packing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02315)
*Zitouni Rania,Mostefai Mounir Sofiane,Tati Youcef,Badaoui Ikram,Bousdjira Nadine,Hasnaoui Sarah*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出RL-HGGA混合算法，将Falkenauer的混合分组遗传算法与表格Q学习控制器集成，通过自适应算子选择在保持接近HGGA求解质量的同时实现50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维装箱问题是NP难的组合优化问题，传统HGGA求解质量高但计算成本大，而启发式方法(如FFD)速度快但质量差。旨在通过强化学习自适应选择遗传算子，在求解质量与计算效率之间取得更好平衡。

Method: 将HGGA与表格Q学习控制器结合；智能体基于8维状态表征(代际进度、停滞水平、最优性差距、平均适应度、种群方差、平均装箱填充率)从8种宏动作(BPCX交叉、轻/重变异、Martello-Toth局部搜索、种群重启等)中动态选择算子；采用epsilon-greedy策略训练400轮，epsilon衰减至0.05。

Result: 在标准基准集(Falkenauer T/U, Scholl 1-3, Hard28)上，RL-HGGA平均最优性差距0.95%，接近HGGA(0.75%)，远优于FFD(2.47%)；平均计算时间从64.22秒降至1.29秒，实现50倍加速。

Conclusion: 学习型自适应算子选择能够以极低的计算成本达到接近HGGA的求解质量，验证了强化学习在组合优化中算子选择的有效性。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a canonical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with broad industrial applications. We propose RL-HGGA, a hybrid algorithm that integrates Falkenauer's Hybrid Grouping Genetic Algorithm (HGGA) with a tabular Q-learning controller. Rather than applying genetic operators at fixed probabilities, a Q-learning agent dynamically selects among eight macro-actions -- including BPCX crossover, light and heavy mutation, Martello-Toth local search, and population restart -- based on an eight-dimensional state representation encoding generation progress, stagnation level, optimality gap, average fitness, population variance, and average bin fill rate. The agent is trained with an epsilon-greedy policy over 400 episodes, with epsilon decaying to 0.05. Experiments on standard benchmark families (Falkenauer T/U, Scholl 1-3, Hard28) show that RL-HGGA achieves an average optimality gap of 0.95% -- competitive with HGGA (0.75%) and well below FFD (2.47%) -- while reducing mean computation time from 64.22 s to 1.29 s, a 50x speedup. These results demonstrate that learned adaptive operator selection can achieve near-HGGA solution quality at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [When Algebraic Symmetry Breaking Meets Solvers: An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01726)
*Madalina Erascu,Johannes Middeke*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 论文实验评估了自动生成的多项式对称性破缺约束在整数线性规划中的效果，发现其有效性高度依赖于求解器，紧凑的二次破缺器族能提升性能，而线性化、大规模破缺器集或求解器重构可能因模型规模增大或搜索行为不利而抵消收益，建议以求解器感知的方式评估对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自动生成的多项式对称性破缺约束在整数线性规划中的实际有效性，特别是其在不同求解器下的表现差异。

Method: 基于ISSAC 2026提出的方法，比较求解器原生二次处理、求解器内部重构和显式线性化三种方式，在近半容量装箱基准问题上进行实验，使用多种数学规划求解器和SMT求解器。

Result: 多项式对称性破缺的有效性高度依赖求解器；紧凑的二次破缺器族可提升性能；线性化、大规模破缺器集或求解器重构会因模型规模增大或搜索行为不利而抵消收益。

Conclusion: 自动生成的对称性破缺约束应以求解器感知的方式进行评估，而非视为与求解器无关的模型附加项。

Abstract: We present an experimental evaluation of automatically generated polynomial symmetry breaking constraints for integer linear programs. Starting from the method that we introduced at the International Symposium on Symbolic and Algebraic Computation (ISSAC) 2026, we compare solver native quadratic handling, solver-internal reformulation, and explicit linearization on near half-capacity bin-packing benchmarks.
  Experiments with several mathematical programming solvers and satisfiability modulo theory solvers show that the effectiveness of polynomial symmetry breaking is strongly solver-dependent.
